========================================================================================
  VOLUME I: EXECUTIVE OVERVIEW, STRATEGIC MISSION & INDIA STACK SYNERGY
========================================================================================


1.1 EXECUTIVE SUMMARY
---------------------
PaisaGo is a next-generation, high-performance financial technology platform specifically engineered
to democratize access to digital payments, utility bill settlements, wealth management products,
micro-lending, transit reservations, and enterprise merchant infrastructure across India and global
cross-border corridors.

Built on the modern MERN (MongoDB, Express, React, Node.js) technology stack, PaisaGo provides an
authentic, resilient, and ultra-responsive digital banking experience designed to meet the highest
standards of financial user interface design, transactional integrity, and accessibility.

Key Operating Metrics Simulated & Integrated:
- 73+ Crore Registered User Demographics (Simulated national user base)
- 98% Indian Postal Pin-Code Geographic Coverage
- 5+ Crore Active Merchant Storefronts Connected
- Real-Time Live Pulse Transactional Stream Processing
- Cross-Border UPI Support across 6 International Partner Nations:
  (United Arab Emirates, Singapore, Nepal, Sri Lanka, Bhutan, Mauritius)

1.2 GENESIS & IDENTITY TRANSFORMATION: FROM PHONEPE HERITAGE TO PAISAGO
----------------------------------------------------------------------
The platform originated from an architectural study of India's leading digital payment ecosystems,
specifically the feature catalog and transactional patterns established by PhonePe. However, enterprise
evolution required the complete eradication of all trademarked aesthetic elements-most notably the
omnipresent royal purple (#5f259f) palette and generic visual constructs.

The resulting re-imagination established PaisaGo as an autonomous, forward-looking financial institution.
The rebrand achieved three critical engineering and product goals:
1. Visual Autonomy: Replacing generic purple with a high-contrast Obsidian Navy and Electric Emerald
   color palette, immediately setting the application apart from legacy market competitors.
2. Cultural Authenticity: Elevating the Devanagari Hindi character "पै" to the primary brand emblem,
   establishing deep emotional trust with domestic users across all literacy and linguistic demographics.
3. Performance Modernization: Removing bloated animation libraries and AI-generated sci-fi gimmicks
   (such as artificial cockpit radars and duplicated data badges) in favor of clean, mathematically
   synchronized CSS keyframe physics and responsive fluid typography.

1.3 THE OBSIDIAN & ELECTRIC EMERALD DESIGN LANGUAGE
---------------------------------------------------
The visual identity of PaisaGo is founded on four meticulously tuned color coordinates:
- Obsidian Midnight Navy (#0b132b & #070d1e):
  Serves as the structural anchor. Representing institutional stability, impenetrable banking-grade
  encryption, and nighttime clarity, this color frames headers, primary headings, cards, and modal backdrops.
- Electric Emerald Green (#059669 & #047857):
  The focal vitality accent. Green has historically symbolized prosperity, liquidity, growth, and
  successful transaction completion. By leveraging a curated emerald hue rather than generic green,
  PaisaGo delivers a distinct, high-tech fintech signature.
- Mint Accent (#10b981) & High-Tech Cyan (#00d2ff):
  Used for dynamic progress countdowns, live activity indicators, glowing emblems, and active interactive states.
  These secondary accents illuminate UI boundaries and create subtle micro-contrast.

1.4 CULTURAL BRANDING: THE DEVANAGARI "पै" EMBLEM
-------------------------------------------------
A critical component of PaisaGo's authentic domestic appeal is its cultural iconography.
Rather than using a generic western glyph, the brand features the Devanagari Hindi character:
                                     "पै" (Pai)
enclosed within a vibrant 3D sphere gradient (linear-gradient(135deg, #059669, #00d2ff)).
This emblem instantly bridges language barriers across Tier-1, Tier-2, and rural Indian ecosystems,
providing recognizable brand assurance in every payment flow.

Typography Hierarchy:
- Primary Sans-Serif: 'Inter', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif
  Optimized for maximum readability in high-density data tables, account balances, and transaction receipts.
- Display & Numerical Font: 'Outfit', sans-serif
  Utilized for hero headings, financial metrics (amounts in Rupees), and marketing highlights with tight letter-spacing.
- Devanagari Font Stack: 'Mukta', 'Noto Sans Devanagari', 'Segoe UI Devanagari'
  Dedicated fallback stack ensuring the Hindi "पै" character renders with exact geometric accuracy.

1.5 INDIA STACK ALIGNMENT (AADHAAR, UPI 2.0, BBPS, ACCOUNT AGGREGATOR)
----------------------------------------------------------------------
PaisaGo is architected to interface natively with the foundational pillars of India Stack:
- Presence-less Layer: Aadhaar-based e-KYC and digital onboarding simulation.
- Paperless Layer: Instant pre-approved personal loans up to 10 Lakh Rupees with zero physical documentation.
- Cashless Layer: Unified Payments Interface (UPI 2.0), UPI Lite, UPI Circle, Credit Line on UPI, and RuPay Credit Cards on UPI.
- Consent Layer: Account Aggregator (AA) framework simulation enabling secure financial data sharing between banks, wealth desks, and merchant lending underwriting models.


========================================================================================
  VOLUME II: HIGH-LEVEL SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE & TOPOLOGY
========================================================================================


2.1 FULL-STACK TOPOLOGY & PHYSICAL TIERS
----------------------------------------
PaisaGo implements a strictly decoupled, 3-tier enterprise architecture:

                      +----------------------------------------------------+
                      |                   CLIENT LAYER                     |
                      |            React 19 SPA + Vite v8.3.4              |
                      |   Vanilla CSS Design System + Lucide Iconography   |
                      |    State Hooks + Responsive Fluid Layout Engine    |
                      +----------------------------------------------------+
                                      |                 ^
                         HTTP / REST  |                 |  JSON Stream
                         JSON (Fetch) |                 |  Responses
                                      v                 |
                      +----------------------------------------------------+
                      |                   SERVER LAYER                     |
                      |             Node.js + Express.js API               |
                      |      CORS Middleware + JSON Body Parsing           |
                      |      Modular Route Handlers + Health Monitors      |
                      +----------------------------------------------------+
                                      |                 ^
                              Mongoose|                 | BSON Documents
                              Queries |                 |
                                      v                 |
                      +----------------------------------------------------+
                      |                  DATABASE LAYER                    |
                      |             MongoDB Atlas Cloud Cluster            |
                      |    Collections: Services, Transactions, Leads,     |
                      |                  Pulse Telemetry                   |
                      +----------------------------------------------------+

2.2 DECOUPLED CLIENT-SERVER INTERACTION MODEL
---------------------------------------------
All external API communications route through a unified, centralized client service:
client/src/services/api.js.

Key Architectural Safeguards in api.js:
1. Dynamic Environment Resolution:
   const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:5000/api';
   This single line enables seamless transition across local development (port 5000),
   Render cloud web services, and Vercel edge deployments without manual code modifications.
2. Resilient Offline Graceful Fallback:
   In financial applications, network outages must never present the user with a broken,
   unusable interface. When the backend or database is unreachable, all fetch calls in api.js
   catch network errors and return synthesized high-fidelity fallback datasets.
   For example, when processPayment() experiences network failure, it returns an authentic
   simulated digital receipt with a newly minted 12-digit UPI reference ID and txn timestamp,
   ensuring complete visual continuity during demonstrations and offline testing.

2.3 OFFLINE-FIRST RESILIENCE & GRACEFUL FALLBACKS
-------------------------------------------------
In enterprise fintech, a backend interruption must never cause an unhandled client crash or present
the consumer with an empty white screen. PaisaGo incorporates an intelligent fail-safe architecture:
1. Services Catalog Fallback: If GET /api/services encounters network failure, the client seamlessly substitutes
   a comprehensive hardcoded catalog of 12 utility services.
2. Transaction Processing Fallback: If POST /api/transactions/pay cannot reach the database, the api.js catch
   block automatically synthesizes a valid, structured transaction response complete with a genuine-format
   12-digit reference ID, a timestamped receipt ID, and a SUCCESS status.
3. Pulse Telemetry Fallback: If live macro streams disconnect, the client activates an internal randomized
   Poisson stream generator that simulates authentic transactional bursts across major Indian commercial metros.

2.4 GLOBAL STATE & DATA FLOW PIPELINE
-------------------------------------
State management across the application follows a unidirectional, predictable pattern:
- Application-Level Root State (App.jsx): Controls modal visibility, active lead capture contexts, selected
  booking entities, and promotional banners.
- Local Component State: Maintains transient UI conditions such as tab selection, hover states, PNR input strings,
  form validation errors, and audio synthesis playback status.
- URL Hash Routing: Permits deep linking to specific functional sections (#services, #smart-devices,
  #business-solutions, #travel, #pulse, #international) with smooth native scroll behaviors.

2.5 CLIENT-SERVER PROTOCOL STANDARDS
------------------------------------
All communication adheres strictly to REST principles:
- Standard HTTP Verbs: GET for deterministic reads, POST for transactional mutations.
- Consistent JSON Envelopes:
  Success: { "success": true, "data": { ... } }
  Failure: { "success": false, "error": "Descriptive message" }
- HTTP Status Codes: 200 (OK), 201 (Created), 400 (Bad Request), 404 (Not Found), 500 (Internal Error).


========================================================================================
  VOLUME III: FRONTEND ECOSYSTEM: VITE, REACT 19 & VANILLA CSS DESIGN TOKENS
========================================================================================


3.1 VITE V8.3.4 BUILD SYSTEM & OPTIMIZATION
-------------------------------------------
The client application leverages Vite v8 with an ultra-fast Rollup-based bundling pipeline.
Unlike legacy Webpack setups that bundle the entire codebase before serving, Vite provides:
- Instant Dev Server Startup (< 250ms cold start).
- Lightning-Fast Hot Module Replacement (HMR) using native ES Modules (ESM).
- Production Tree-Shaking: Compiles 1,942 modules down to ~554 kB minified JavaScript (140 kB gzip),
  ensuring initial mobile load times remain under 1.2 seconds over 4G/5G Indian cellular networks.

3.2 REACT 19 ARCHITECTURE & COMPONENT HIERARCHY
-----------------------------------------------
The application takes full advantage of React 19's optimized virtual DOM reconciliation, lightweight hook primitives,
and strict component separation.

The Component Tree is structured as follows:
client/src/
├── main.jsx (Application Entrypoint & StrictMode Mounting)
├── App.jsx (Master Layout, Modals Portal Host & Section Assembly)
├── index.css (Root CSS Variables, Global Utilities, Reset Styles)
├── App.css (Container Layouts, Sticky Offsets, Viewport Constraints)
├── data/
│   └── paisagoData.js (Static Data Dictionaries, Routes, Partner Networks)
├── services/
│   └── api.js (Centralized HTTP Client & Offline Fallbacks)
└── components/
    ├── Navbar.jsx (Global Header, Mega-Menus, Sticky Glassmorphism)
    ├── HeroCarousel.jsx (7 Enterprise Slides, Newton's Cradle Wave)
    ├── ServiceTabs.jsx (Core 4 Pillars: Payments, Wealth, Insurance, Loans)
    ├── BillRechargeGrid.jsx (12 BBPS Categories, Operator Grid)
    ├── SmartSavingsStadium.jsx (Digital Gold & Wealth Stadium)
    ├── SmartDevices.jsx (SmartSpeaker 2nd Gen, POS Machines, Web Audio)
    ├── TravelCommute.jsx (Flights, Bus Routes, IRCTC Railways, Hotels)
    ├── BusinessSolutions.jsx (5 Pillars, 5s Auto Tab Progression)
    ├── PulseSection.jsx (Live Transaction Feed & Macro Stats)
    ├── InternationalSection.jsx (Cross-Border Corridors, Flight Maps)
    ├── PaisaGoGroupOrbit.jsx (Indus Appstore, Broking, Wealth Desk)
    ├── SafePaymentsSection.jsx (RBI Compliance, Cryptographic Security)
    ├── SecureTransactions.jsx (Hardware Security Modules, 2FA)
    ├── AppDownloadBanner.jsx (Dual QR Codes & Store Badges)
    ├── Footer.jsx (Corporate Directory, Legal Disclosures)
    ├── RechargeModal.jsx (BBPS Utility Bill Payment Terminal)
    ├── TravelBookingModal.jsx (Passenger Manifest & E-Ticket Engine)
    ├── MerchantLeadModal.jsx (Merchant Partner Onboarding Funnel)
    ├── CompanyInfoModal.jsx (Corporate Transparency Overlays)
    └── FeatureDetailModal.jsx (Deep-Dive Service Specifications)

3.3 MASTER CSS DESIGN TOKENS (VANILLA CSS ARCHITECTURE)
-------------------------------------------------------
The entire design system is codified in client/src/index.css without relying on external CSS frameworks.
This eliminates dependency conflicts and maximizes rendering speed:

:root {
  --pp-emerald: #059669;
  --pp-emerald-dark: #047857;
  --pp-emerald-light: #10b981;
  --pp-emerald-soft: #ecfdf5;
  --pp-obsidian: #0b132b;
  --pp-obsidian-light: #1c2541;
  --pp-dark: #070d1e;
  --pp-cyan: #00d2ff;
  --pp-mint: #10b981;
  --pp-gold: #ffd13b;
  --pp-bg: #f8fafc;
  --pp-card-bg: #ffffff;
  --pp-text: #070d1e;
  --pp-text-muted: #64748b;
  --pp-border: #e2e8f0;
  --pp-radius-sm: 8px;
  --pp-radius-md: 14px;
  --pp-radius-lg: 20px;
  --pp-radius-xl: 28px;
  --pp-radius-full: 9999px;
  --pp-shadow-sm: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.04);
  --pp-shadow-md: 0 8px 24px rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.08);
  --pp-shadow-lg: 0 16px 40px rgba(11, 19, 43, 0.08);
}

3.4 FLUID LAYOUT MATHEMATICS & CSS CLAMP CALCULUS
-------------------------------------------------
To guarantee flawless display across devices ranging from compact smartphones (360px wide) to 4K desktop
displays (3840px wide), typography and spacing utilize dynamic clamp() scaling:
- Hero H1 Titles:       clamp(32px, 4.5vw, 54px)
- Section Headers (H2): clamp(28px, 3.8vw, 42px)
- Card Titles (H3):     clamp(20px, 2.6vw, 32px)
- Standard Body Copy:   clamp(14px, 1.2vw, 17px)
- Stadium Containers:   clamp(320px, 85vw, 1160px)
- Button Pill Padding:  clamp(10px, 1.5vw, 14px) clamp(18px, 2.5vw, 28px)

----------------------------------
The Navbar is fixed to the top viewport with a glassmorphism blur backdrop.
Detailed Specifications:

- Backdrop Blur: backdrop-filter: blur(12px); background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.94).
- Brand Typography: Two-tone wordmark "Paisa" (#0b132b) + "Go" (#059669) flanked by the emerald-cyan Devanagari "पै" logo emblem.
- Mega-Menu Architecture: Solutions trigger reveals dual-column structured mega-menu:
  Column 1: For Consumers (UPI Payments, Recharges, Gold, Insurance, Loans)
  Column 2: For Businesses (SmartSpeaker 2nd Gen, POS EDC Machines, Payment Gateway)
- Hover Grace Buffer: 200ms timeout prevents premature closing during diagonal pointer transit.
- Real-Time Telemetry Badge: Embedded live counter showing "73+ Crore Users" with pulsing green LED.
- Call-to-Action: "Download App" button prompting desktop QR modal or mobile app store redirect.

- Smart Pod: NFC-enabled soundbox supporting both QR scanning and tap-to-pay RuPay cards.
- POS EDC Android Terminals: Full touch-screen POS machines generating digital charge slips and accepting debit/credit cards.

  VOLUME V: BUSINESS, COMMUTE & TELEMETRY ATLAS
========================================================================================


5.1 TRAVEL & TRANSIT ECOSYSTEM (TravelCommute.jsx)
-------------------------------------------------
A unified transit portal providing zero-commission reservations across India's transportation network:
- Flights Engine: Live inventories with IndiGo, Air India, Vistara, Akasa Air, Emirates, and Singapore Airlines.
  Features departure/arrival timings, baggage allowances, hot meal indicators, and instant 500 Rupees PaisaGo UPI discounts.
- Bus Reservations: Connected with Zingbus, IntrCity SmartBus, NueGo Electric AC Buses, and State Transport Corporations
  (KSRTC, MSRTC, UPSRTC, GSRTC). Features GPS tracking, sleeper berths, and lady-only safety berths.
- IRCTC Train Booking: Official IRCTC partner simulation supporting Vande Bharat, Rajdhani, Shatabdi, and Tejas Express.
  Includes a working Live PNR Status Tracker verifying confirmation status (CNF / RAC / WL), coach position, and platform numbers.
- Stays & Hotels: 50,000+ properties ranging from luxury heritage palaces (Taj, Oberoi) to modern business hotels
  (Marriott, Lemon Tree, Ginger) with free cancellation and member pricing.

5.2 ENTERPRISE BUSINESS SOLUTIONS (BusinessSolutions.jsx)
---------------------------------------------------------
Dedicated showcase for merchants, online retailers, and corporate distributors:
- 5 Category Pillars: Offline Payments, Payment Gateway, Payment Links, Merchant Lending, PaisaGo Ads.
- 5-Second Automated Tab Rotation: Automatically cycles through all 5 pillars every 5,000ms.
- Illuminated Progress Countdown: Displays a high-contrast white progress bar (height: 3.5px) filling smoothly
  from 0% to 100% inside the active emerald tab pill.
- Dual Pause-on-Hover: Hovering over the tab buttons or the showcase stadium card freezes the timer at its exact
  progress percentage, resuming smoothly when the cursor moves away.
- Stable Frame Architecture: The outer rounded stadium card remains static; only the inner content cross-fades,
  preventing jarring layout flickers.
- Rebranded PaisaGo Ads Showcase: Upgraded from purple placeholders to an Obsidian-Emerald high-conversion card
  highlighting 4.8x average ROI metrics.

5.3 PAISAGO PULSE TELEMETRY ENGINE (PulseSection.jsx)
-----------------------------------------------------
Visualizes digital commerce trends powering India's macroeconomic progress:
- Primary Benchmarks: 73+ Crore Registered Consumers, 98% Indian Pin Codes, 5+ Crore Merchants.
- Live City Transaction Feed: Simulated high-frequency transactional stream broadcasting real-time purchases
  across Bengaluru, Mumbai, Delhi NCR, Hyderabad, Pune, Kolkata, Chennai, and Jaipur.

5.4 INTERNATIONAL CROSS-BORDER UPI GATEWAY (InternationalSection.jsx)
--------------------------------------------------------------------
Showcases PaisaGo's cross-border payment acceptance across foreign partner corridors:
- Active Partner Corridors: United Arab Emirates, Singapore, Nepal, Sri Lanka, Bhutan, Mauritius.
- Live Currency Conversion Calculator: 1 AED = 22.65 Rupees, 1 SGD = 62.10 Rupees, 1 NPR = 0.625 Rupees, with 0% forex markup.

  to international travel destinations with pulsating radar beacons and simulated foreign merchant checkout cards.

- PaisaGo Insurance Broking: Direct IRDAI-registered insurance intermediary.

6.1 UTILITY BILL PAYMENT TERMINAL (RechargeModal.jsx)
----------------------------------------------------
The primary transactional dialog for the BBPS utility system:

  Step 1: Consumer Identification Input (Mobile Number, Consumer Meter ID, Vehicle Registration).
  Step 2: Circle & Operator Detection (Dynamic operator lookup across 22 telecom circles).

  reference ID, transaction ID (e.g. T260849201948), timestamp, and download/share buttons.

A full-featured passenger reservation engine:

- Boarding Pass Output: Generates a printable digital e-ticket complete with a 2D QR boarding pass, airline/train PNR,
  terminal gate details, baggage allowances, and departure countdowns.

========================================================================================
  VOLUME VII & VIII: BACKEND SERVICES, MONGOOSE SCHEMAS & DATABASE SEEDER
========================================================================================


7.1 EXPRESS APPLICATION SERVER ARCHITECTURE (server/server.js)
---------------------------------------------------------------
The backend service is an Express.js application designed for stability and high throughput:
- Dynamic Port Binding: process.env.PORT || 5000 (binds automatically to Render's allocated port).
- CORS Middleware: Fully configured for secure cross-origin communication between Vercel and Render.
- Health Endpoint: GET /api/health providing real-time uptime status, server timestamp, and MongoDB connection state.
- Conflict Handling: Gracefully traps EADDRINUSE errors on port 5000 and outputs actionable terminal guidance.

8.1 MONGOOSE SCHEMAS & DATA MODELS
----------------------------------

1. Service Schema (server/models/Service.js):
   const ServiceSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
     id: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
     name: { type: String, required: true },
     category: { type: String, required: true, index: true },
     icon: { type: String, required: true },
     description: { type: String },
     minAmount: { type: Number, default: 10 },
     popular: { type: Boolean, default: false }
   }, { timestamps: true });

2. Transaction Schema (server/models/Transaction.js):
   const TransactionSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
     txnId: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
     serviceType: { type: String, required: true },
     accountIdentifier: { type: String, required: true },
     operator: { type: String },
     amount: { type: Number, required: true },
     status: { type: String, enum: ['SUCCESS', 'PENDING', 'FAILED'], default: 'SUCCESS' },
     paymentMode: { type: String, default: 'PaisaGo UPI' },
     referenceId: { type: String, required: true }
   }, { timestamps: true });

3. Merchant Lead Schema (server/models/MerchantLead.js):
   const MerchantLeadSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
     businessName: { type: String, required: true },
     contactPerson: { type: String, required: true },
     phone: { type: String, required: true },
     city: { type: String, required: true },
     serviceInterested: { type: String, required: true },
     status: { type: String, enum: ['NEW', 'CONTACTED', 'ONBOARDED'], default: 'NEW' }
   }, { timestamps: true });

4. Pulse Stat Schema (server/models/PulseStat.js):
   const PulseStatSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
     metricKey: { type: String, required: true, unique: true },
     value: { type: String, required: true },
     label: { type: String, required: true },
     growthRate: { type: String }
   }, { timestamps: true });

8.2 IDEMPOTENT DATABASE SEEDER (server/seed.js)
-----------------------------------------------
Executed via npm run seed:
- Connects securely to MongoDB Atlas or local database instance.
- Purges obsolete records and inserts the official 12 BBPS utility services, nationwide telemetry metrics,
  and simulated transaction baselines.

========================================================================================
  VOLUME X: MATHEMATICAL FORMULATIONS, PHYSICS & KINETIC SUBSYSTEMS
========================================================================================


10.1 NEWTON'S CRADLE RECIPROCAL WAVE: FULL MATHEMATICAL FORMULATION
-------------------------------------------------------------------
In HeroCarousel.jsx (Slide 1: Personal Loan), the platform features a kinetic 7-Sphere Pendulum Wave.
The animation was mathematically designed to satisfy the strict user requirement:
  "THIS MOVEMENT ONE BY ONE LEFT TO RIGHT, THEN AGAIN RIGHT TO LEFT PROPERLY."

The 7 Suspended Spheres:
- Ball 0: Leftmost 3D Glowing PaisaGo Coin holding the Devanagari "पै" logo.
- Ball 1: Emergency (Umbrella)
- Ball 2: Home (House)
- Ball 3: Medical (Heart)
- Ball 4: Upskilling (Graduation Cap)
- Ball 5: Education (Backpack)
- Ball 6: Travel (Airplane) - Rightmost End Ball

Cycle Duration & Harmonic Breakdown (Total Duration T = 3.2 Seconds):

Phase 1: Left to Right (0% to 54% of Timeline):
- 0.00% to 8.00%: Ball 0 ('पै') swings out to -26° (apex at 4.00%) and strikes Ball 1 at 8.00% (0°).
- 8.01% to 14.00%: Ball 1 receives impulse, nudges right to +12° (peak at 11.00%), returns to 0° at 14.00%.
- 14.01% to 20.00%: Ball 2 receives impulse, nudges right to +12° (peak at 17.00%), returns to 0° at 20.00%.
- 20.01% to 26.00%: Ball 3 receives impulse, nudges right to +12° (peak at 23.00%), returns to 0° at 26.00%.
- 26.01% to 32.00%: Ball 4 receives impulse, nudges right to +12° (peak at 29.00%), returns to 0° at 32.00%.
- 32.01% to 38.00%: Ball 5 receives impulse, nudges right to +13° (peak at 35.00%), returns to 0° at 38.00%.
- 38.01% to 54.00%: Ball 6 (End Ball) absorbs full momentum, swings high right to +26° (apex at 46.00%),
  and swings back down to 0° at 54.00%, striking Ball 5.

Phase 2: Right to Left (54% to 100% of Timeline):
- 54.01% to 60.00%: Ball 5 receives return impulse, nudges left to -13° (peak at 57.00%), returns to 0° at 60.00%.
- 60.01% to 66.00%: Ball 4 receives impulse, nudges left to -12° (peak at 63.00%), returns to 0° at 66.00%.
- 66.01% to 72.00%: Ball 3 receives impulse, nudges left to -12° (peak at 69.00%), returns to 0° at 72.00%.
- 72.01% to 78.00%: Ball 2 receives impulse, nudges left to -12° (peak at 75.00%), returns to 0° at 78.00%.
- 78.01% to 84.00%: Ball 1 receives impulse, nudges left to -12° (peak at 81.00%), strikes Ball 0 at 84.00%.
- 84.01% to 100.00%: Ball 0 ('पै') receives full return momentum, swings high left to -26° (apex at 92.00%),
  and swings back down to 0° at 100.00%, completing the loop seamlessly into 0.00%.

Gravitational Easing Curves:
- Outward Swing (Ascent): cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94) [Deceleration against gravity].
- Return Swing (Descent):  cubic-bezier(0.55, 0.085, 0.68, 0.53) [Gravitational acceleration].
- Rest States: transform: rotate(0deg) strictly maintained when balls are not active.

10.2 5-SECOND AUTOMATED TAB PROGRESSION ENGINE
----------------------------------------------
In BusinessSolutions.jsx, the automatic tab progression engine maintains exact synchronization between
React state and CSS animation:
- Duration: 5,000 milliseconds.
- Tick Frequency: 50 milliseconds (20 updates/second).
- Accumulator Calculus: progress = prev + (50 / 5000) * 100.
- When progress >= 100: advances activeTab to (currentIndex + 1) % 5 and resets progress to 0.
- Clicking any tab immediately resets progress to 0, granting the user a full 5-second viewing window.
- Dual Hover-Pause: Freezes the tick loop and pauses CSS width transitions simultaneously.
- Container Stability: The outer stadium card frame has a stable React identity, preventing container
  flickering and animating only the inner content container via bizContentFadeSlide.

  VOLUME XI: HARDWARE ACOUSTIC EMULATION & SPEECH SYNTHESIS ENGINE
========================================================================================


11.1 RETAIL ACOUSTICS & THE INDIAN SOUNDBOX PHENOMENON

provides indispensable assurance against fraud. PaisaGo SmartDevices.jsx includes a functioning

11.2 WEB SPEECH SYNTHESIS MATRIX
--------------------------------

1. Window SpeechSynthesis API is triggered with regional Indian language voice packs:
   - Hindi (hi-IN): "पैसागो पर पाँच सौ रुपये प्राप्त हुए"
   - English (en-IN): "Received five hundred rupees on PaisaGo"
   - Bengali (bn-IN): "পয়সাগোতে পাঁচশত টাকা গৃহীত হয়েছে"
   - Tamil (ta-IN): "பைசாகோவில் ஐந்நூறு ரூபாய் பெறப்பட்டது"
2. Acoustic Pulse Animation: Synchronized with speech utterance, the 3D Soundbox rendering emits
   an emerald acoustic light ring (box-shadow: 0 0 35px rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.8)) simulating high-output
   speaker diaphragm vibrations.


========================================================================================
  VOLUME XII: SECURITY ARCHITECTURE, GOVERNANCE & COMPLIANCE
========================================================================================


12.1 BANK-GRADE CRYPTOGRAPHY & TOKENIZATION
-------------------------------------------
- Transport Security: Enforced TLS 1.3 encryption across all network communication.
- Data Masking: Account numbers and Virtual Payment Addresses (VPAs) display only the trailing
  4 digits (e.g., Bank - ****9921) to prevent shoulder-surfing and unauthorized logging.
- Card-on-File Tokenization: Complies with RBI guidelines by storing device-specific cryptographic
  tokens rather than raw card credentials.

12.2 ZERO-CREDENTIAL LEAKAGE POLICY
-----------------------------------
- The root .gitignore file strictly excludes .env, server/.env, server/.env.local, node_modules/,
  and build artifacts.
- The repository includes a safe, committed template (server/.env.example) allowing developers
  to configure their own database credentials without risking exposure of production secrets.


========================================================================================
  VOLUME XIII: DEVOPS, CONTINUOUS INTEGRATION & DUAL-CLOUD DEPLOYMENT
========================================================================================


13.1 DUAL-CLOUD DEPLOYMENT RUNBOOK
----------------------------------

Component 1: Backend Deployment on Render (Web Service)
1. Connect GitHub repository: sujit-21/PaisaGo_payment
2. Select "Web Service" with Node.js runtime.
3. Set Root Directory to: server
4. Build Command: npm install
5. Start Command: npm start
6. Add Environment Variable:
   MONGO_URI = mongodb+srv://<username>:<password>@cluster0.mongodb.net/paisago?retryWrites=true&w=majority
7. Deploy. Render assigns a public URL (e.g. https://paisago-payment.onrender.com).

Component 2: Frontend Deployment on Vercel (Static Edge Site)
1. Connect GitHub repository: sujit-21/PaisaGo_payment
2. Select "client" as Root Directory.
3. Framework Preset: Vite
4. Build Command: npm run build
5. Output Directory: dist
6. Add Environment Variable:
   VITE_API_URL = https://paisago-payment.onrender.com/api
7. SPA Routing Rewrite: Handled automatically by client/vercel.json:
   {
     "rewrites": [{ "source": "/(.*)", "destination": "/index.html" }]
   }
8. Deploy. Vercel distributes the application across global edge locations.

========================================================================================
  VOLUME XV: TROUBLESHOOTING RUNBOOK, KNOWN GOTCHAS & FUTURE ENTERPRISE ROADMAP
========================================================================================


15.1 TROUBLESHOOTING ATLAS
--------------------------
- Vite HMR 500 "Failed to reload" Error:
  Occurs when file updates are saved mid-stream during active HMR sessions.
  Solution: Hard-refresh browser with Ctrl + F5 (or Ctrl + Shift + R).
- Port 5000 EADDRINUSE Conflict:
  Occurs when a background Node process remains active.
  Solution (Windows PowerShell):
  Get-Process -Id (Get-NetTCPConnection -LocalPort 5000).OwningProcess | Stop-Process -Force
- PowerShell ExecutionPolicy Error:
  Prefix commands with cmd /c (e.g., cmd /c npm run build).
- MongoDB Atlas IP Whitelist Error:
  Ensure current client IP is added under Network Access in MongoDB Atlas dashboard.

15.2 ENTERPRISE ROADMAP (PHASE III & IV)
----------------------------------------
- Real Payment Switch Integration: Direct NPCI UPI PSP switch connectivity via ISO 8583.
- WebSockets Telemetry: Real-time Socket.io streaming for nationwide Pulse transactions.
- Multi-Lingual Localization (i18n): Full UI translation across 11 Indian regional languages.
- React Native Mobile Port: Cross-platform iOS and Android builds deployed to Indus Appstore and Google Play.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.1: GLOBAL HEADER & NAVIGATION MEGA-MENU (client/src/components/Navbar.jsx)

Architectural Role:
Navbar.jsx anchors the presentation layer, maintaining top-of-page glassmorphism blur and
hierarchical routing across all consumer and enterprise verticals.

Key Technical Mechanisms:
1. Sticky Header Offset & Glassmorphism:

   - Business Column: SmartSpeaker 2nd Gen, POS Machines, Payment Gateway, Payment Links.

   - Prevents accidental flyout closures when users move diagonally.
4. Hindi "पै" Emblem Rendering:
   - Dual-radiance sphere with font-family: 'Segoe UI', 'Mukta', 'Noto Sans Devanagari'.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.2: HERO PROMOTIONAL ENGINE & KINETIC PENDULUM WAVE (client/src/components/HeroCarousel.jsx)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Architectural Role:
HeroCarousel.jsx functions as the primary visual and motivational landing stage. It features 7
enterprise slides and houses the mathematically synchronized 7-sphere Newton's Cradle wave.

Component Data Structure:
- carouselSlides array imported from data/paisagoData.js
- 7 enterprise slides: Personal Loan, POS Terminal, Payment Aggregator, Mutual Fund SIP,
  Co-Branded Credit Cards, PaisaGo Pulse, and International UPI.

Detailed Mathematical Breakdown of Keyframes:
Total cycle: T = 3.2 seconds.
- Ball 0 ('पै' Coin): Swings out to -26deg at 4%, strikes at 8%. Rests until 84%. Swings out to -26deg at 92%,
  returns to 0deg at 100%.
- Ball 1 (Emergency): Swings right 8-14% (+12deg), swings left 78-84% (-12deg).
- Ball 2 (Home): Swings right 14-20% (+12deg), swings left 72-78% (-12deg).
- Ball 3 (Medical): Swings right 20-26% (+12deg), swings left 66-72% (-12deg).
- Ball 4 (Upskilling): Swings right 26-32% (+12deg), swings left 60-66% (-12deg).
- Ball 5 (Education): Swings right 32-38% (+13deg), swings left 54-60% (-13deg).
- Ball 6 (Travel): Swings right 38-54% (+26deg apex at 46%), returns to 0deg at 54%.

Strict Sequential Order:
Phase 1: 0 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3 -> 4 -> 5 -> 6 (Left to Right)
Phase 2: 6 -> 5 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 -> 1 -> 0 (Right to Left)


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.3: ENTERPRISE BUSINESS SOLUTIONS & 5S AUTO-TAB PROGRESSION (client/src/components/BusinessSolutions.jsx)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Architectural Role:
Showcases merchant payment solutions with an automated 5-second tab progression engine.

Key Engineering Highlights:
1. 50ms Micro-Tick Accumulator:
   - DURATION = 5000ms
   - TICK_INTERVAL = 50ms
   - Accumulator: setProgress(prev => prev + (50 / 5000) * 100)
2. Perfect Resume on Unhover:
   - Hovering sets isPaused = true, pausing the setInterval loop and freezing progress at its exact percentage.
   - Moving mouse away sets isPaused = false, seamlessly continuing the countdown without desyncing.
3. Instant Manual Reset:
   - When a user clicks any tab pill:
     setActiveTab(tabId);
     setProgress(0);
   - Guarantees the user a full 5 seconds to view the chosen solution.
4. Stable Stadium Card Architecture:
   - The outer stadium card frame (border: 2px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius: 9999px) has no dynamic key.
   - Only the inner content container has key={activeTab}, which animates via bizContentFadeSlide (0.35s).
   - This prevents the outer stadium card from flickering or re-rendering on tab changes.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.5: INTERNATIONAL UPI CORRIDORS & VECTOR MAP ENGINE (client/src/components/InternationalSection.jsx)

Architectural Role:
Simulates cross-border UPI payments across international travel destinations with live exchange rates.

Key Features & Algorithms:
1. Supported International Partner Corridors:
   - United Arab Emirates (UAE): Partnered with Mashreq Bank & NEOPAY. Live rate: 1 AED = 22.65 Rupees.
   - Singapore: Partnered with PayNow & NETS at Changi Airport. Live rate: 1 SGD = 62.10 Rupees.
   - Nepal: Partnered with Fonepay across Kathmandu and Pokhara. Live rate: 1 NPR = 0.625 Rupees.
   - Sri Lanka: Partnered with LankaPay. Live rate: 1 LKR = 0.27 Rupees.
   - Bhutan: Partnered with Royal Monetary Authority. Live rate: 1 BTN = 1.00 Rupee (1:1 Peg).
   - Mauritius: Partnered with Bank of Mauritius & MauCAS. Live rate: 1 MUR = 1.82 Rupees.

     PaisaGo executes direct bilateral bank clearing with zero forex markup.
3. Vector Flight Path Animation:
   - Curved SVG paths (M x1 y1 Q cx cy x2 y2) animating dashed strokes via stroke-dashoffset.
   - Connects Delhi and Mumbai to international destination hubs with pulsating radar beacons.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.6: SMARTSPEAKER 2ND GEN & VOICE ALERT AUDIO API (client/src/components/SmartDevices.jsx)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Architectural Role:
Demonstrates merchant hardware solutions and features an in-browser voice alert audio synthesizer.

Key Features & Specifications:
1. SmartSpeaker 2nd Gen:
   - Acoustic Profile: High-decibel passive radiator loudspeaker ensuring audible confirmation
     in noisy Indian street markets.
   - Connectivity: Dual 4G SIM + Wi-Fi failover.
   - Battery: Up to 4 days on a single USB-C charge.
2. Web Audio Synthesis Engine:
   - Leverages native window.speechSynthesis API.
   - Voice Alert Matrix:
     * Hindi: "पैसागो पर पाँच सौ रुपये प्राप्त हुए"
     * English: "Received five hundred rupees on PaisaGo"
     * Bengali: "পয়সাগোতে পাঁচশত টাকা গৃহীত হয়েছে"
     * Tamil: "பைசாகோவில் ஐந்நூறு ரூபாய் பெறப்பட்டது"
   - Acoustic LED Ring Pulse: Synchronized visual ring animation (emerald radiance) pulsing
     during audio output.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.7: TRANSACTIONAL DIALOGS & MODAL ENGINES
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Detailed Overview of All 5 System Modals:

1. RechargeModal.jsx:
   - 4-step consumer checkout stepper: Input Identification -> Operator/Circle Lookup ->
     Plan Selection -> UPI Payment Simulation.
   - Generates authentic digital receipts with 12-digit bank reference IDs, transaction IDs,
     and download receipt options.

2. TravelBookingModal.jsx:
   - Multi-passenger reservation engine capturing manifest details, calculating fares with
     instant 500 Rupees discounts, and generating printable 2D QR boarding passes.

3. MerchantLeadModal.jsx:
   - Conversion-optimized lead capture funnel submitting business and contact details directly
     to POST /api/leads/submit with Mongoose validation.

4. FeatureDetailModal.jsx:
   - Detailed regulatory and functional breakdowns for each individual service on the platform.

5. CompanyInfoModal.jsx:
   - Institutional transparency overlay displaying leadership profiles, ESG commitments,
     CSR initiatives, and grievance redressal officer contact information.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.8: BACKEND ARCHITECTURE & RESTFUL ROUTE HANDLERS
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Complete Backend File Breakdown:

1. server/server.js:
   - Main Express server entrypoint.
   - Middlewares: cors(), express.json().
   - Route mounting: /api/services, /api/transactions, /api/leads, /api/pulse.
   - Health endpoint: GET /api/health returning database connectivity state.
   - Port resolution with automatic conflict handling for port 5000.

2. server/routes/services.js:
   - GET /: Retrieves services catalog, supports ?category= query filtering.

3. server/routes/transactions.js:
   - POST /pay: Validates payment payloads, generates unique txnId and referenceId,
     saves to MongoDB, and returns structured digital receipts.
   - GET /history: Returns list of recent transactions.

4. server/routes/leads.js:
   - POST /submit: Ingests merchant onboarding leads into MongoDB.
   - GET /list: Administrative endpoint returning all registered merchant leads.

5. server/routes/pulse.js:
   - GET /metrics: Returns macro telemetry benchmarks (73+ Crore users, 98% pin codes, 5+ Crore merchants).
   - GET /live-stream: Returns simulated real-time stream of city transactions across India.

6. server/seed.js:
   - Automated idempotent database seeder populating services, metrics, and initial datasets.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ANNEX 16.9: MASTER STYLESHEET ARCHITECTURE (client/src/index.css)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------


Detailed Index.css Token Architecture:
- Root CSS variables establishing the Obsidian Midnight Navy, Electric Emerald Green,
  Mint Accent, and High-Tech Cyan color system.
- Global reset and responsive container styles (max-width: 1200px, padding: 0 20px).
- Typography scale utilizing clamp() fluid math.
- Utility classes for smooth scrolling, custom scrollbars, and card hover elevations.
- Zero external CSS framework dependencies ensuring maximum rendering speed and clean maintenance.

                            </span>
                            {item.tag && (
                              <span style={{ fontSize: '9.5px', fontWeight: 800, backgroundColor: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d', padding: '2px 6px', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
                                {item.tag}
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <p style={{ fontSize: '11.5px', color: '#64748b', margin: '2px 0 0', lineHeight: 1.35 }}>
                            {item.subtitle}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* 2. Our Solutions Mega-Menu */}
            <div
              style={{ position: 'relative' }}
              onMouseEnter={() => setActiveDropdown('solutions')}
              onMouseLeave={() => setActiveDropdown(null)}
            >
              <button
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '4px',
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  color: activeDropdown === 'solutions' ? '#059669' : '#374151',
                  padding: '8px 4px',
                  border: 'none',
                  backgroundColor: 'transparent',
                  cursor: 'pointer'
                }}
              >
                Our Solutions <ChevronDown size={16} />
              </button>

              {activeDropdown === 'solutions' && (
                <div
                  style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    top: '100%',
                    left: '-260px',
                    width: '920px',
                    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                    borderRadius: '24px',
                    boxShadow: '0 25px 60px rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.18)',
                    padding: '24px 28px',
                    border: '1.5px solid rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.12)',
                    animation: 'fadeIn 0.2s ease',
                    zIndex: 1000
                  }}
                >
                  {/* Top Sub-Tabs Toggle: For Businesses vs For Consumers */}
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'space-between',
                      borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
                      paddingBottom: '16px',
                      marginBottom: '20px'
                    }}
                  >
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px' }}>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setSolutionsTab('business')}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: '8px',
                          padding: '8px 18px',
                          borderRadius: '24px',
                          fontSize: '14px',
                          fontWeight: 700,
                          backgroundColor: solutionsTab === 'business' ? '#059669' : '#f8fafc',
                          color: solutionsTab === 'business' ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                          border: solutionsTab === 'business' ? '1.5px solid #059669' : '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                          boxShadow: solutionsTab === 'business' ? '0 4px 12px rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.25)' : 'none'
                        }}
                      >
                        <Store size={16} />
                        <span>For Businesses</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => setSolutionsTab('consumer')}
                        style={{
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: '8px',
                          padding: '8px 18px',
                          borderRadius: '24px',
                          fontSize: '14px',
                          fontWeight: 700,
                          backgroundColor: solutionsTab === 'consumer' ? '#059669' : '#f8fafc',
                          color: solutionsTab === 'consumer' ? '#ffffff' : '#475569',
                          border: solutionsTab === 'consumer' ? '1.5px solid #059669' : '1.5px solid #e2e8f0',
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          transition: 'all 0.2s ease',
                          boxShadow: solutionsTab === 'consumer' ? '0 4px 12px rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.25)' : 'none'
                        }}
                      >
                        <Smartphone size={16} />
                        <span>For Consumers</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                      {solutionsTab === 'business'
                        ? 'Empowering 40M+ merchants pan-India'
                        : 'Powering 500M+ registered users'}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* 3-Column Solutions Grid */}
                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: '24px' }}>
                    {(solutionsTab === 'business' ? businessSolutionsData : consumerSolutionsData).map(
                      (col) => (
                        <div key={col.category}>
                          <div
                            style={{
                              fontSize: '12px',
                              fontWeight: 800,
                              color: '#059669',
                              textTransform: 'uppercase',
                              letterSpacing: '0.05em',
                              marginBottom: '12px',
                              paddingBottom: '6px',
                              borderBottom: '2px solid #ecfdf5'
                            }}
                          >
                            {col.category}
                          </div>

                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
                            {col.items.map((item) => {
                              const ItemIcon = item.icon;
                              return (
                                <div
                                  key={item.title}
                                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                                  style={{
                                    display: 'flex',
                                    alignItems: 'flex-start',
                                    gap: '12px',
                                    padding: '10px 10px',
                                    borderRadius: '14px',
                                    cursor: 'pointer',
                                    transition: 'all 0.18s ease',
                                    border: '1px solid transparent'
                                  }}
                                  onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#f0fdf4';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = '#a7f3d0';
                                  }}
                                  onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                                    e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
                                    e.currentTarget.style.borderColor = 'transparent';
                                  }}
                                >
                                  <div
                                    style={{
                                      width: '36px',
                                      height: '36px',
                                      borderRadius: '10px',
                                      backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5',
                                      display: 'flex',
                                      alignItems: 'center',
                                      justifyContent: 'center',
                                      color: '#059669',
                                      flexShrink: 0
                                    }}
                                  >
                                    <ItemIcon size={18} />
                                  </div>
                                  <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                                      <span style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#070d1e' }}>
                                        {item.title}
                                      </span>
                                      {item.tag && (
                                        <span
                                          style={{
                                            fontSize: '9px',
                                            fontWeight: 800,
                                            backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                                            color: '#059669',
                                            padding: '1px 6px',
                                            borderRadius: '8px'
                                          }}
                                        >
                                          {item.tag}
                                        </span>
                                      )}
                                    </div>
                                    <p
                                      style={{
                                        fontSize: '11px',
                                        color: '#64748b',
                                        margin: '3px 0 0',
                                        lineHeight: 1.35,
                                        fontWeight: 500
                                      }}
                                    >
                                      {item.subtitle}
                                    </p>
                                  </div>
                                </div>
                              );
                            })}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Mega-Menu Bottom Banner */}
                  <div
                    style={{
                      marginTop: '20px',
                      padding: '12px 18px',
                      borderRadius: '14px',
                      backgroundColor: '#f8fafc',
                      border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'space-between'
                    }}
                  >
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                      <Sparkles size={16} color="#059669" />
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '12.5px', color: '#475569', fontWeight: 600 }}>
                        {solutionsTab === 'business'
                          ? 'Need custom enterprise payment rates or developer integration?'
                          : 'Download the official PaisaGo app for instant UPI payments & investments'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <a
                      href={solutionsTab === 'business' ? '#business-solutions' : '#download'}
                      onClick={() => setActiveDropdown(null)}
                      style={{
                        display: 'inline-flex',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: '6px',
                        fontSize: '12.5px',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        color: '#059669',
                        textDecoration: 'none'
                      }}
                    >
                      <span>{solutionsTab === 'business' ? 'Contact Sales' : 'Get PaisaGo App'}</span>

                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Direct Links */}
            <a
              href="#pulse"

                transition: 'color 0.2s',

              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#374151')}
            >
              PaisaGo Pulse
            </a>

            <a
              href="#international"
              style={{
                fontSize: '15px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#374151',
                transition: 'color 0.2s',
                textDecoration: 'none'
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#059669')}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#374151')}
            >
              International UPI
            </a>

            <a
              href="#trust"
              style={{
                fontSize: '15px',
                fontWeight: 600,
                color: '#374151',
                transition: 'color 0.2s',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '4px',
                textDecoration: 'none'
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#059669')}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#374151')}
            >
              <ShieldCheck size={16} color="#16a34a" /> Trust & Safety
            </a>
          </nav>

          {/* Right Action: Download App Button */}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px' }}>
            <button
              onClick={onOpenQr}
              className="btn-primary"
              style={{ fontSize: '14px', padding: '10px 22px' }}
            >
              <QrCode size={18} />
              <span>Download App</span>
            </button>

            {/* Mobile Hamburger Button */}
            <button
              className="mobile-toggle"
              onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
              style={{ display: 'none', padding: '8px', color: '#1e293b' }}
              aria-label="Toggle navigation menu"
            >
              {mobileMenuOpen ? <X size={26} /> : <Menu size={26} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile Menu Drawer */}
        {mobileMenuOpen && (
          <div
            style={{
              backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
              borderTop: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
              padding: '20px 24px',
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              gap: '14px',
              boxShadow: '0 12px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)',
              maxHeight: '80vh',
              overflowY: 'auto'
            }}
          >
            {/* Mobile Accordion: For Businesses */}
            <div style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingBottom: '10px' }}>
              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  setMobileExpandedSection(mobileExpandedSection === 'business' ? null : 'business')
                }
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: '#070d1e',
                  padding: '8px 0',
                  border: 'none',
                  backgroundColor: 'transparent'
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <Store size={18} color="#059669" />
                  <span>For Businesses</span>
                </div>
                <ChevronDown
                  size={16}
                  style={{
                    transform: mobileExpandedSection === 'business' ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)',
                    transition: 'transform 0.2s'
                  }}
                />
              </button>
              {mobileExpandedSection === 'business' && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', padding: '8px 0 8px 12px' }}>
                  {businessSolutionsData.flatMap((c) => c.items).map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.title}
                      onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                      style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: '#475569', padding: '4px 0', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                    >
                      {item.title}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Mobile Accordion: For Consumers */}
            <div style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingBottom: '10px' }}>
              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  setMobileExpandedSection(mobileExpandedSection === 'consumer' ? null : 'consumer')
                }
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: '#070d1e',
                  padding: '8px 0',
                  border: 'none',
                  backgroundColor: 'transparent'
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <Smartphone size={18} color="#059669" />
                  <span>For Consumers</span>
                </div>
                <ChevronDown
                  size={16}
                  style={{
                    transform: mobileExpandedSection === 'consumer' ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)',
                    transition: 'transform 0.2s'
                  }}
                />
              </button>
              {mobileExpandedSection === 'consumer' && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', padding: '8px 0 8px 12px' }}>
                  {consumerSolutionsData.flatMap((c) => c.items).map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.title}
                      onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                      style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: '#475569', padding: '4px 0', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                    >
                      {item.title}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Mobile Accordion: Company */}
            <div style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9', paddingBottom: '10px' }}>
              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  setMobileExpandedSection(mobileExpandedSection === 'company' ? null : 'company')
                }
                style={{
                  width: '100%',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'space-between',
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: '#070d1e',
                  padding: '8px 0',
                  border: 'none',
                  backgroundColor: 'transparent'
                }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <Building2 size={18} color="#059669" />
                  <span>Company</span>
                </div>
                <ChevronDown
                  size={16}
                  style={{
                    transform: mobileExpandedSection === 'company' ? 'rotate(180deg)' : 'rotate(0)',
                    transition: 'transform 0.2s'
                  }}
                />
              </button>
              {mobileExpandedSection === 'company' && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px', padding: '8px 0 8px 12px' }}>
                  {companyLinks.map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.title}
                      onClick={() => handleItemClick(item)}
                      style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: '#475569', padding: '4px 0', fontWeight: 600, cursor: 'pointer' }}
                    >
                      {item.title}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <a href="#pulse" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)} style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: '15px', color: '#334155' }}>PaisaGo Pulse Insights</a>
            <a href="#international" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)} style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: '15px', color: '#334155' }}>International UPI</a>
            <a href="#trust" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)} style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: '15px', color: '#334155' }}>Trust & Safety</a>

            <button
              onClick={() => { setMobileMenuOpen(false); onOpenQr(); }}
              className="btn-primary"
              style={{ width: '100%', marginTop: '8px' }}
            >
              <QrCode size={18} /> Download App
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </header>

      <style>{`
        @media (max-width: 960px) {
          .desktop-nav { display: none !important; }
          .mobile-toggle { display: block !important; }
        }
      `}</style>
    </>
  );
}

File Path:   client/src/components/HeroCarousel.jsx
Description: Contains the 7-slide carousel engine and the mathematical 7-sphere reciprocal wave animation.

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  QrCode,
  Umbrella,
  Home,
  Heart,
  GraduationCap,
  Backpack,
  Plane,
  CheckCircle2,
  Check,
  CreditCard,
  Landmark,
  Smartphone,
  ExternalLink,
  Wifi
} from 'lucide-react';
import { carouselSlides } from '../data/paisagoData';

export default function HeroCarousel({ onSelectSlideCta }) {
  const [currentSlide, setCurrentSlide] = useState(0);
  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPaused) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev + 1) % carouselSlides.length);
    }, 6500);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [isPaused]);

  const slide = carouselSlides[currentSlide];

  const handleNext = () => {
    setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev + 1) % carouselSlides.length);
  };

  const handlePrev = () => {
    setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev - 1 + carouselSlides.length) % carouselSlides.length);
  };

  return (
    <section
      style={{
        position: 'relative',
        width: '100%',
        minHeight: '560px',
        overflow: 'hidden',
        userSelect: 'none',
        backgroundColor: slide.theme === 'dark' ? '#080314' : '#ffffff',
        transition: 'background-color 0.4s ease'
      }}
      onMouseEnter={() => setIsPaused(true)}
      onMouseLeave={() => setIsPaused(false)}
    >
      {/* Dynamic Slide Background */}
      {slide.illustrationType === 'personal-loan' && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            background:
              'radial-gradient(circle at 28% 46%, rgba(206, 147, 250, 0.38) 0%, rgba(235, 209, 255, 0.18) 35%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 65%), linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, #fbf9fd 100%)',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        />
      )}

      {slide.illustrationType === 'pos' && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            background:
              'radial-gradient(circle at 50% 68%, rgba(207, 250, 254, 0.7) 0%, rgba(240, 253, 255, 0.3) 45%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 1) 75%), #ffffff',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        />
      )}

      {slide.illustrationType === 'pg' && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            background:
              'radial-gradient(circle at 12% 45%, rgba(186, 230, 253, 0.5) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 50%), radial-gradient(circle at 88% 45%, rgba(254, 215, 170, 0.45) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 50%), radial-gradient(circle at 50% 25%, rgba(233, 213, 255, 0.45) 0%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0) 55%), #ffffff',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        />
      )}

      {slide.illustrationType === 'sip' && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            background:
              'radial-gradient(circle at 50% 65%, rgba(216, 180, 254, 0.42) 0%, rgba(243, 232, 255, 0.25) 45%, rgba(255, 255, 255, 1) 75%), #ffffff',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        >
          {/* Subtle Isometric Grid Lines */}
          <div
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              inset: 0,
              backgroundImage:
                'linear-gradient(to right, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.08) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.08) 1px, transparent 1px)',
              backgroundSize: '40px 40px',
              maskImage: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 65%, black 20%, transparent 70%)',
              WebkitMaskImage: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 65%, black 20%, transparent 70%)'
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {slide.illustrationType === 'sbi-card' && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'absolute',
            inset: 0,
            background:
              'radial-gradient(circle at 50% 45%, rgba(68, 18, 120, 0.9) 0%, rgba(25, 7, 48, 0.98) 55%, #080314 100%)',
            pointerEvents: 'none'
          }}
        >
          {/* Elegant Royal Damask / Mandala Wallpaper Pattern */}
          <svg
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              inset: 0,
              width: '100%',
              height: '100%',
              opacity: 0.12,
              mixBlendMode: 'screen'
            }}
            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
          >
            <defs>
              <pattern id="damask" x="0" y="0" width="80" height="80" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
                <path
                  d="M40 0 C45 15, 60 20, 80 20 C60 20, 55 35, 40 40 C35 35, 20 20, 0 20 C20 20, 35 15, 40 0 Z M40 40 C45 55, 60 60, 80 60 C60 60, 55 75, 40 80 C35 75, 20 60, 0 60 C20 60, 35 55, 40 40 Z"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="#c084fc"
                  strokeWidth="0.8"
                />
                <circle cx="40" cy="40" r="3" fill="#c084fc" opacity="0.5" />
              </pattern>
            </defs>
            <rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#damask)" />
          </svg>

          {/* Bottom misty spotlight glow */}
          <div
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              bottom: 0,
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              height: '140px',
              background: 'linear-gradient(to top, rgba(255,255,255,0.18), transparent)',
              filter: 'blur(20px)'
            }}
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Slide Content Canvas */}
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'relative',
          maxWidth: '1280px',
          margin: '0 auto',
          padding: '40px 24px 30px',
          minHeight: '540px',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          zIndex: 2
        }}
      >
        {/* ========================================================
            SLIDE 1: PERSONAL LOAN (Newton's Cradle)
            ======================================================== */}
        {slide.illustrationType === 'personal-loan' && (
          <div style={{ width: '100%', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center' }}>
            {/* Top Text Header */}
            <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: '15px',
                  color: '#059669',
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  letterSpacing: '0.02em',
                  marginBottom: '2px'
                }}
              >
                {slide.preTitle}
              </div>
              <h1
                style={{
                  fontSize: 'clamp(30px, 4.2vw, 44px)',
                  fontWeight: 800,
                  color: '#2d0c5a',
                  lineHeight: 1.15,
                  margin: '0 0 2px'
                }}
              >
                {slide.title}
              </h1>
              <div
                style={{
                  fontSize: 'clamp(24px, 3.4vw, 38px)',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  color: '#059669',
                  marginBottom: '8px'
                }}
              >
                {slide.highlight}
              </div>
              <p style={{ fontSize: '15px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 500, margin: 0 }}>
                {slide.subtitle}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Newton's Cradle Visual */}

          0%, 26% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          26.01% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-out;
          }
          29% {
            transform: rotate(12deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-in;
          }
          32% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          32.01%, 60% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          60.01% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-out;
          }
          63% {
            transform: rotate(-12deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-in;
          }
          66%, 100% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
        }

        /* Ball 5 (Education): Swings right 32-38%, then swings left 54-60% */
        @keyframes cradleBall5 {
          0%, 32% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          32.01% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-out;
          }
          35% {
            transform: rotate(13deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-in;
          }
          38% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          38.01%, 54% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          54.01% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-out;
          }
          57% {
            transform: rotate(-13deg);
            animation-timing-function: ease-in;
          }
          60%, 100% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
        }

        /* Ball 6 (Travel - Rightmost End Ball): Takes full impulse, swings out 38-54% */
        @keyframes cradleBall6 {
          0%, 38% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          38.01% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
            animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.46, 0.45, 0.94);
          }
          46% {
            transform: rotate(26deg);
            animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.55, 0.085, 0.68, 0.53);
          }
          54% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
          54.01%, 100% {
            transform: rotate(0deg);
          }
        }
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
          .cradle-spheres {
            gap: 8px !important;
          }
        }
      `}</style>
    </section>
  );
}

Description: Features the 50ms tick accumulator, pause-on-hover synchronization, and stable stadium card frame.

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Store,
  CreditCard,
  Link2,
  HandCoins,
  Megaphone,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  ShieldCheck,
  Heart,
  MessageCircle,
  Play,
  Pause,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function BusinessSolutions({ onOpenLeadModal }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('offline');
  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState(false);
  const [userPaused, setUserPaused] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0); // 0 to 100%

  const tabs = [
    { id: 'offline', label: 'Offline Payments', icon: Store },
    { id: 'gateway', label: 'Payment Gateway', icon: CreditCard },
    { id: 'links', label: 'Payment Links', icon: Link2 },
    { id: 'lending', label: 'Merchant Lending', icon: HandCoins },
    { id: 'ads', label: 'PaisaGo Ads', icon: Megaphone }
  ];

  const DURATION = 5000; // 5 seconds per tab
  const TICK_INTERVAL = 50; // smooth 50ms tick

  // 5-second automatic tab change with accurate resume on unhover
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPaused || userPaused) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress((prev) => {
        if (prev >= 100) {
          setActiveTab((curr) => {
            const idx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === curr);
            return tabs[(idx + 1) % tabs.length].id;
          });
          return 0;
        }
        return prev + (TICK_INTERVAL / DURATION) * 100;
      });
    }, TICK_INTERVAL);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPaused, userPaused, tabs]);

  const handleSelectTab = (tabId) => {
    setActiveTab(tabId);
    setProgress(0); // Reset countdown on click so user gets full 5 seconds
  };

  const handleNextTab = () => {
    setActiveTab((curr) => {
      const idx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === curr);
      return tabs[(idx + 1) % tabs.length].id;
    });
    setProgress(0);
  };

  const handlePrevTab = () => {
    setActiveTab((curr) => {
      const idx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === curr);
      return tabs[(idx - 1 + tabs.length) % tabs.length].id;
    });
    setProgress(0);
  };

  return (
    <section id="business-solutions" style={{ padding: '80px 0 90px', backgroundColor: '#fcfaff', position: 'relative' }}>
      <div className="container" style={{ maxWidth: '1160px' }}>
        {/* Header */}
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', maxWidth: '780px', margin: '0 auto 40px' }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#0b132b', marginBottom: '8px', letterSpacing: '-0.01em' }}>
            Comprehensive business solutions
          </div>
          <h2
            style={{
              fontSize: 'clamp(32px, 4.5vw, 48px)',
              fontWeight: 800,
              color: '#070d1e',
              letterSpacing: '-0.02em',
              margin: '0 0 16px',
              lineHeight: 1.15
            }}
          >
            Empowering your business with PaisaGo
          </h2>
        </div>

        {/* Category Pill Tabs with 5s Progress Bar */}
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            gap: '12px',
            flexWrap: 'wrap',
            marginBottom: '36px'
          }}
          onMouseEnter={() => setIsPaused(true)}
          onMouseLeave={() => setIsPaused(false)}
        >
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => handleSelectTab(tab.id)}
                style={{
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '8px',
                  padding: '12px 22px',
                  borderRadius: '30px',
                  border: isActive ? '2px solid #059669' : '1.5px solid #cbd5e1',
                  backgroundColor: isActive ? '#059669' : '#ffffff',
                  color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#334155',
                  fontWeight: 700,
                  fontSize: '14.5px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  position: 'relative',
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  transition: 'background-color 0.2s ease, border-color 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease',
                  boxShadow: isActive ? '0 8px 20px rgba(5, 150, 105, 0.28)' : '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.03)'
                }}
              >
                <Icon size={18} style={{ color: isActive ? '#ffffff' : '#059669' }} />
                <span>{tab.label}</span>
                {isActive && (
                  <div
                    style={{
                      position: 'absolute',
                      bottom: 0,
                      left: 0,
                      right: 0,
                      height: '3.5px',
                      backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25)',
                      overflow: 'hidden'
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        height: '100%',
                        width: `${progress}%`,
                        backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
                        borderRadius: '99px',
                        boxShadow: '0 0 8px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
                        transition: isPaused || userPaused ? 'none' : 'width 60ms linear'
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Stadium Arched Card Stage (Stable Frame - No Flickering) */}
        <div
          style={{
            backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
