          onMouseLeave={() => setIsPaused(false)}
        >
          {/* Animated Tab Content Container */}
          <div
            key={activeTab}

            style={{

              cursor: 'pointer',
              border: 'none'
            }}
          >
            Join Partner Network
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`

        }

            opacity: 0;
            transform: translateY(10px);
          }

          }

            width: 100% !important;
            height: 260px !important;
          }
        }

}

  const playVoiceSample = (lang = 'hi') => {
    setPlaying(true);
    if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      let text = 'PaisaGo par ₹500 prapt hue!';
      if (lang === 'en') text = '₹500 received on PaisaGo!';
      if (lang === 'bn') text = 'PaisaGo-te ₹500 joma hoyechhe!';
      if (lang === 'ta') text = 'PaisaGo-yil ₹500 perappattathu!';

      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.rate = 0.95;
      utterance.pitch = 1.05;
      utterance.onend = () => setPlaying(false);
      utterance.onerror = () => setPlaying(false);
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    } else {
      setTimeout(() => setPlaying(false), 2400);
    }
  };

                  <Check size={28} strokeWidth={3.5} />

              {/* Physical Keypad Grid */}
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)', gap: '4px', padding: '4px 0' }}>
                {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((k) => (
                  <div key={k} style={{ height: '10px', backgroundColor: '#94a3b8', borderRadius: '2px' }} />
                ))}

            {/* Interactive Plus Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveModalDevice(devices[2])}
              style={{
                width: '38px',
                height: '38px',
                borderRadius: '50%',
                backgroundColor: '#0b132b',
                color: '#ffffff',
                border: '2.5px solid #ffffff',
                boxShadow: '0 6px 16px rgba(11, 19, 43,  0.45)',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                fontSize: '22px',
                fontWeight: 700,
                cursor: 'pointer',
                marginTop: '-18px',

                transition: 'transform 0.2s ease, background-color 0.2s ease'
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1.15)'; e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#ffd13b'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#0b132b'; }}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => { e.currentTarget.style.transform = 'scale(1)'; e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = '#0b132b'; e.currentTarget.style.color = '#ffffff'; }}
              title="View EDC Machine details"
            >

            </button>

            <div style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#070d1e', marginTop: '10px' }}>
              EDC Machine
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Device 4: PaisaGo QR Standee (Bottom Center) */}
          <div
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              left: '46%',
              bottom: '2%',
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              alignItems: 'center',
              zIndex: 12,
              transition: 'transform 0.3s ease'
            }}
            className="device-isometric-unit"
          >
            {/* Isometric Tilted QR Standee */}
            <div
              style={{
                width: '140px',
                height: '170px',

                border: '2.5px solid #047857',
                borderRadius: '16px',
                boxShadow: '0 18px 30px rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.2)',

                पै PaisaGo

              <div style={{ padding: '6px', backgroundColor: '#ffffff', borderRadius: '8px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                <svg width="60" height="60" viewBox="0 0 60 60">
                  <rect x="2" y="2" width="18" height="18" rx="2" fill="#059669" />
                  <rect x="5" y="5" width="12" height="12" rx="1" fill="#ffffff" />
                  <rect x="8" y="8" width="6" height="6" fill="#059669" />

                  <rect x="40" y="2" width="18" height="18" rx="2" fill="#059669" />
                  <rect x="43" y="5" width="12" height="12" rx="1" fill="#ffffff" />
                  <rect x="46" y="8" width="6" height="6" fill="#059669" />

                  <rect x="2" y="40" width="18" height="18" rx="2" fill="#059669" />

              onClick={() => setActiveModalDevice(devices[3])}

                width: '38px',

      {activeModalDevice && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'fixed',
            inset: 0,
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(18, 7, 35, 0.75)',

            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',

          }}

        >
          <div
            style={{
              backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
              borderRadius: '28px',
              padding: '36px',
              maxWidth: '480px',
              width: '100%',
              boxShadow: '0 25px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)',
              position: 'relative'
            }}
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <button
              onClick={() => setActiveModalDevice(null)}
              style={{
                position: 'absolute',
                top: '20px',
                right: '20px',
                width: '36px',
                height: '36px',
                borderRadius: '50%',
                backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9',
                color: '#475569',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center'
              }}
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>

            <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5', color: '#059669', padding: '4px 12px', borderRadius: '12px', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: '10px' }}>
              <Sparkles size={14} />
              {activeModalDevice.badge}
            </div>

            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '24px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#070d1e', margin: '0 0 4px' }}>
              {activeModalDevice.name}
            </h3>

            <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#059669', marginBottom: '14px' }}>
              {activeModalDevice.subtitle}
            </div>

            <p style={{ fontSize: '14.5px', color: '#475569', lineHeight: 1.55, marginBottom: '20px' }}>
              {activeModalDevice.desc}
            </p>

            <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8fafc', borderRadius: '16px', padding: '16px', marginBottom: '24px' }}>
              <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#070d1e', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                Key Technical Specifications:
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
                {activeModalDevice.specs.map((spec, i) => (
                  <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px', color: '#334155' }}>
                    <Check size={16} color="#16a34a" style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '2px' }} />
                    <span>{spec}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
              <div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#64748b', fontWeight: 600 }}>PRICING</div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '14.5px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#070d1e' }}>{activeModalDevice.price}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveModalDevice(null);
                onOrderDevice && onOrderDevice(activeModalDevice);
              }}
              style={{
                width: '100%',
                backgroundColor: '#059669',
                color: '#ffffff',
                padding: '14px',
                borderRadius: '24px',
                fontWeight: 800,
                fontSize: '15px',
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                gap: '8px',
                boxShadow: '0 10px 25px rgba(5, 150, 105,  0.3)'
              }}
            >
              <span>Order Device for My Business</span>
              <ArrowRight size={18} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <style>{`
        @keyframes floatToken {
          0% { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) rotateX(60deg) translateY(0); }
          100% { transform: translate(-50%, -50%) rotateX(60deg) translateY(-8px); }
        }
        @media (max-width: 900px) {
          .isometric-hardware-arena {
            height: auto !important;
            flex-direction: column !important;
            gap: 40px !important;
            padding: 40px 0 !important;
          }
          .device-isometric-unit {
            position: relative !important;
            left: auto !important;
            right: auto !important;
            top: auto !important;
            bottom: auto !important;
          }
        }
      `}</style>
    </section>
  );
}

                      <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 800, backgroundColor: '#dcfce7', color: '#15803d', padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
                        {pnrResult.status}
                      </span>

                        {pnrResult.coach} - {pnrResult.seat}

                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Trains Grid */}
              <div
                style={{
                  display: 'grid',
                  gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(310px, 1fr))',
                  gap: '16px'
                }}
              >
                {irctcTrains.map((train, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    style={{
                      backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)',
                      backdropFilter: 'blur(8px)',
                      borderRadius: '16px',
                      padding: '18px',
                      border: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15)',
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      justifyContent: 'space-between',
                      gap: '14px'
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '15px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#ffffff' }}>
                          {train.name}
                        </span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '10.5px', fontWeight: 800, backgroundColor: '#ffd13b', color: '#12131a', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '10px' }}>

                        {train.route}
                      </div>

                        {train.departure} → {train.arrival} ({train.duration})
                      </div>

                      <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: '#40c1d3', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>

                        <span>{train.food}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)', paddingTop: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>

                        <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)' }}>Fare from</div>

                          ₹{train.price.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => onBookTravel({
                          type: 'train',

                        })}
                        style={{

                          color: '#10213b',
                          border: 'none',
                          padding: '9px 18px',
                          borderRadius: '20px',
                          fontSize: '12.5px',
                          fontWeight: 800,
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: '6px'
                        }}
                      >
                        <span>Book Train</span>
                        <ArrowRight size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB CONTENT: 4. STAYS & HOTELS */}
          {activeTab === 'hotels' && (
            <div>
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '18px' }}>

                </span>
              </div>

                style={{
                  display: 'grid',
                  gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(310px, 1fr))',

                        {hotel.property}
                      </div>

                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '5px', fontSize: '11.5px', color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
                        <MapPin size={13} color="#40c1d3" />
                        <span>{hotel.location}</span>
                      </div>

                      <div style={{ fontSize: '11.5px', color: '#40c1d3', fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                        {hotel.perk}
                      </div>

                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                        {hotel.amenities.map((am, i) => (
                          <span key={i} style={{ fontSize: '10px', backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '8px', color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)' }}>
                            {am}
                          </span>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1)', paddingTop: '12px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
                      <div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)' }}>Per Night From</div>
                        <div style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: 800, color: '#ffd13b' }}>
                          ₹{hotel.price.toLocaleString('en-IN')}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={() => onBookTravel({
                          type: 'hotel',
                          company: hotel.company,
                          title: `${hotel.company} - ${hotel.property}`,
                          location: hotel.location,
                          price: hotel.price
                        })}
                        style={{
                          backgroundColor: '#40c1d3',
                          color: '#10213b',
                          border: 'none',
                          padding: '9px 18px',
                          borderRadius: '20px',
                          fontSize: '12.5px',
                          fontWeight: 800,
                          cursor: 'pointer',
                          display: 'inline-flex',
                          alignItems: 'center',
                          gap: '6px'

                        <span>Reserve Room</span>
                        <ArrowRight size={14} />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <style>{`
        @media (max-width: 768px) {
          .travel-box-inner {
            padding: 24px 16px !important;
          }
        }
      `}</style>
    </section>
  );
}

========================================================================================
  VOLUME XXIV: ANNOTATED CODEBASE - CLIENT API CLIENT & OFFLINE FALLBACKS (CLIENT/SRC/SERVICES/API.JS)
========================================================================================

File Path:   client/src/services/api.js
Description: Dynamic API resolution with VITE_API_URL and resilient graceful fallbacks for offline reliability.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  COMPLETE SOURCE CODE
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:5000/api';

export async function fetchServices(category = '') {
  try {
    const url = category ? `${API_BASE}/services?category=${category}` : `${API_BASE}/services`;
    const res = await fetch(url);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Network error');
    const json = await res.json();
    return json.data;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('API fallback for services:', err.message);
    return null; // triggers local fallback in components
  }
}

export async function processPayment(paymentData) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/transactions/pay`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(paymentData)
    });
    const json = await res.json();
    return json;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('API fallback for payment:', err.message);
    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        txnId: 'T26' + Math.floor(100000000000 + Math.random() * 900000000000),
        serviceType: paymentData.serviceType,
        accountIdentifier: paymentData.accountIdentifier,
        operator: paymentData.operator || '',
        amount: Number(paymentData.amount),
        status: 'SUCCESS',
        paymentMode: paymentData.paymentMode || 'PaisaGo UPI',
        referenceId: Math.floor(100000000000 + Math.random() * 900000000000).toString(),
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      }
    };
  }
}

export async function submitMerchantLead(leadData) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/leads/submit`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(leadData)
    });
    const json = await res.json();
    return json;
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Thank you! A PaisaGo Business representative will contact you shortly.'
    };
  }
}

export async function fetchPulseMetrics() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/pulse/metrics`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Pulse fetch error');
    const json = await res.json();
    return json.data;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function fetchLivePulseStream() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/pulse/live-stream`);
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Live stream error');
    const json = await res.json();
    return json.data;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END OF FILE: client/src/services/api.js


========================================================================================
  VOLUME XXV: ANNOTATED CODEBASE - EXPRESS APPLICATION SERVER (SERVER/SERVER.JS)
========================================================================================

File Path:   server/server.js
Description: Express setup, CORS middleware, route mounting, port conflict resolution, and health diagnostics.


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  COMPLETE SOURCE CODE
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const mongoose = require('mongoose');
require('dotenv').config();

const servicesRoute = require('./routes/services');
const transactionsRoute = require('./routes/transactions');
const leadsRoute = require('./routes/leads');
const pulseRoute = require('./routes/pulse');

const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;
const MONGO_URI = process.env.MONGO_URI || 'mongodb://127.0.0.1:27017/paisago';

// Middlewares
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());

// Routes
app.use('/api/services', servicesRoute);
app.use('/api/transactions', transactionsRoute);
app.use('/api/leads', leadsRoute);
app.use('/api/pulse', pulseRoute);

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    status: 'ONLINE',
    service: 'PaisaGo MERN API',
    database: mongoose.connection.readyState === 1 ? 'CONNECTED' : 'DISCONNECTED / IN-MEMORY FALLBACK',
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  });
});

// Connect to MongoDB & Start Server
async function startServer() {
  try {
    await mongoose.connect(MONGO_URI);
    console.log('✅ Connected to MongoDB successfully.');
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('⚠️ MongoDB connection warning (will use resilient fallback):', err.message);
  }

  const server = app.listen(PORT, () => {
    console.log(`🚀 PaisaGo Backend Server running on port ${PORT}`);
  });

  server.on('error', (err) => {
    if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
      console.error(`⚠️ Port ${PORT} is already in use by another process.`);
      console.log(`💡 You can either free port ${PORT} or change the PORT in .env.`);
      process.exit(1);
    } else {
      console.error('Server error:', err);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  });
}

startServer();


------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END OF FILE: server/server.js

async function seed() {
  try {
    await mongoose.connect(MONGO_URI);
    console.log('Connected to MongoDB for seeding...');

    await Service.deleteMany({});
    await Service.insertMany(servicesData);
    console.log(`Seeded ${servicesData.length} PaisaGo services.`);

    await PulseStat.deleteMany({});
    await PulseStat.create(pulseData);
    console.log('Seeded PaisaGo Pulse statistics.');

    console.log('Seed completed successfully!');
    process.exit(0);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Seed error:', err);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

if (require.main === module) {
  seed();
}

module.exports = { servicesData, pulseData };
